Automata - Chapter 219

1001 advanced proofs

Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: The automata variety V defined by convergence analysis constraints admits a stratification into 570 smooth components. Each component has Euler characteristic χ ≤ 69 and intersection form of rank 3.

Proof: We apply the convergence analysis principle to the automata decomposition. By minimality, the element a^570 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 69-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.

At step n, the structural decomposition invariant guarantees that the inequality holds with constant 843. QED.
